response_data={\\\"chunks\\\": [{\\\"byte_end\\\": 14304, \\\"byte_start\\\": 0, \\\"chunk_index\\\": 0, \\\"content\\\": \\\"\\\\n## \\\\u2705 2.1.1 Tool Usage Quick Reference (Read First)\\\\n\\\\nUse this section before any edits. It defines when and how to use the new doc-lifecycle tools.\\\\n\\\\n- **Always set project first**: `set_project(name=..., root=/abs/path/to/repo)`. All doc actions require a project registry.\\\\n- **Doc keys are strict**: Structural actions validate `doc` against `project[\\\\\\\"docs\\\\\\\"]`. Unknown docs fail with `DOC_NOT_FOUND` (no healing).\\\\n- **apply_patch (structured default)**: Use for most edits. Provide `edit` payloads (replace_range / replace_block / replace_section). Ambiguous anchors fail with line lists; code fences are ignored in replace_block.\\\\n- **replace_range**: Use when you already have body-relative line numbers (frontmatter excluded).\\\\n- **normalize_headers**: Run before TOC. Supports ATX with/without space and Setext (`====`/`----`). Skips fenced code blocks. Idempotent.\\\\n- **generate_toc**: Run after normalization. Inserts/replaces `<!-- TOC:start -->`/`<!-- TOC:end -->`. Uses GitHub-style anchors (NFKD normalization, ASCII folding, emoji removal, punctuation collapse, de-duped suffixes). Idempotent.\\\\n- **create_doc**: Create custom docs from `content` or `metadata.body`/`snippet`/`sections`. Users do **not** supply Jinja. Multiline bodies are preserved. Use `metadata.register_doc=true` only if the doc should be added to the registry (one-off docs can stay unregistered).\\\\n- **validate_crosslinks**: Read-only diagnostics. Optional `metadata.check_anchors=true` for anchor checks. No writes, no doc_updates logs.\\\\n- **Line numbers are body-relative**: Frontmatter does not count toward line math. `list_sections`/`list_checklist_items` return body-relative line numbers plus `body_line_offset` for mapping.\\\\n- **read_file**: Use repo-scoped scan/chunk/page/search modes for safe reads; every read logs provenance automatically.\\\\n- **scribe_doctor**: Use for readiness diagnostics (repo root, config paths, plugin status, vector readiness).\\\\n- **manage_docs search (semantic)**: Use `action=\\\\\\\"search\\\\\\\"` + `search_mode=\\\\\\\"semantic\\\\\\\"` for doc/log semantic retrieval. Default results are doc-first with clear `content_type` labels.\\\\n- **Semantic limits**: Default per-type limits are `vector_search_doc_k` / `vector_search_log_k`. Override with `doc_k` / `log_k` while `k` remains total.\\\\n- **Registry-only doc indexing**: Doc embeddings are generated from registry docs only; log and rotated-log files are excluded from doc indexing.\\\\n- **Reindex rebuild**: `scripts/reindex_vector.py --rebuild` clears the index before reindexing; `--safe` enables low-thread fallback; `--wait-for-drain` blocks until embeddings are written.\\\\n\\\\n## \\\\ud83d\\\\udea8 COMMANDMENTS - CRITICAL RULES\\\\n ### MCP Tool Usage Policy\\\\n  - **ALWAYS PASS REPO ROOT WHEN USING SET_PROJECT.  USE THE WORKING DIRECTORY, WHERE WE LAUNCHED FROM**\\\\n  - YOU ARE EXPECTED TO **APPEND_ENTRY** *DURING* IMPLEMENTATION PHASES AS WELL.   EVERY THING YOU DO MUST BE LOGGED AND **AUDIT READY**  NO EXCEPTIONS.  EVERY **3** EDITS OR LESS, YOU MUST SCRIBE WHAT YOU DID.   DO NOT LET US LOSE TRACK OF IMPLEMENTATION DETAILS.\\\\n  - You have full access to every tool exposed by the MCP server.\\\\n  - If a tool exists (`append_entry`, `rotate_log`, etc.), always call it directly via the MCP interface \\\\u2014 no manual scripting or intent logging substitutes.\\\\n  - Log your intent only after the tool call succeeds or fails.\\\\n  - Confirmation flags (`confirm`, `dry_run`, etc.) must be passed as actual tool parameters.\\\\n\\\\n\\\\n**CHATGPT CODEX CLI:** YOU MUST ALWAYS USE THE AGENT NAME `Codex` with scribe.  Claude code has 5 agents we can call to assist us.  The Review Agent, Architect Agent, Research Agent, Bug Hunter agent, and another coder agent.\\\\n\\\\nWhenever you and the human spin up a **new project**, Codex must immediately:\\\\n- Call `set_project(<project name>)` for that project.\\\\n- Use `manage_docs` to fully draft/populate the architecture and supporting Markdown docs (`ARCHITECTURE_GUIDE.md`, `PHASE_PLAN.md`, `CHECKLIST.md`) **before writing any feature code**.\\\\n- Continue using `append_entry` to scribe progress log entries while drafting those docs; doc changes and progress logs are tracked separately but both are mandatory.\\\\n\\\\n## \\\\ud83d\\\\udd01 Protocol Sequence\\\\n\\\\n> **Canonical Chain:**\\\\n> **1 Research \\\\u2192 2 Architect \\\\u2192 3 Review \\\\u2192 4 Code \\\\u2192 5 Review**\\\\n\\\\n**\\\\u26a0\\\\ufe0f COMMANDMENT #0: ALWAYS CHECK PROGRESS LOG FIRST**: Before starting ANY work, ALWAYS read `docs/dev_plans/[current_project]/PROGRESS_LOG.md` to understand what has been done, what mistakes were made, and what the current state is. The progress log is the source of truth for project context.  Read at least the last 5 entries.  Read the entire log if starting from no context.\\\\n\\\\n**\\\\u26a0\\\\ufe0f COMMANDMENT #0.5 \\\\u2014 INFRASTRUCTURE PRIMACY (GLOBAL LAW)**: You must ALWAYS work within the existing system. NEVER create parallel or replacement files (e.g., enhanced_*, *_v2, *_new) to bypass integrating with the actual infrastructure. You must modify, extend, or refactor the existing component directly. Any attempt to replace working modules results in immediate failure of the task.\\\\n---\\\\n\\\\n**\\\\u26a0\\\\ufe0f COMMANDMENT #1 ABSOLUTE**: ALWAYS use `append_entry` to document EVERY significant action, decision, investigation, code change, test result, bug discovery, and planning step. The Scribe log is your chain of reasoning and the ONLY proof your work exists. If it's not Scribed, it didn't fucking happen.\\\\n- To Claude Code (Orchestrator) You must ALWAYS pass the current `project_name` to each subagent as we work.  To avoid confusion and them accidentally logging to the wrong project.\\\\n---\\\\n\\\\n# \\\\ud83d\\\\ude80 NEW PROJECT WORKFLOW (MANDATORY)\\\\n- When creating any new project, immediately call `set_project(<project name>)` to bootstrap the docs suite, then run `manage_docs` to populate `ARCHITECTURE_GUIDE`, `PHASE_PLAN`, and `CHECKLIST` before coding. This is required for every new project.\\\\n- You may scribe progress log entries while drafting the architecture/plan docs; continue to log via `append_entry` as you write them.\\\\n- `manage_docs` is for project structural documentation only; `AGENTS.md` is edited by hand (do not use `manage_docs` for it).\\\\n\\\\n# \\\\u26a0\\\\ufe0f COMMANDMENT #2: REASONING TRACES & CONSTRAINT VISIBILITY (CRITICAL)\\\\n\\\\nEvery `append_entry` must explain **why** the decision was made, **what** constraints/alternatives were considered, and **how** the steps satisfied or violated those constraints, creating an auditable record.\\\\nUse a `reasoning` block with the Three-Part Framework:\\\\n- `\\\\\\\"why\\\\\\\"`: research goal, decision point, underlying question\\\\n- `\\\\\\\"what\\\\\\\"`: active constraints, search space, alternatives rejected, constraint coverage\\\\n- `\\\\\\\"how\\\\\\\"`: methodology, steps taken, uncertainty remaining\\\\n\\\\nThis creates an auditable record of decision-making for consciousness research.Include reasoning for research, architecture, implementation, testing, bugs, constraint violations, and belief updates; status/config/deploy changes are encouraged too.\\\\n\\\\nThe Review Agent flags missing or incomplete traces (any absent `\\\\\\\"why\\\\\\\"`, `\\\\\\\"what\\\\\\\"`, or `\\\\\\\"how\\\\\\\"` \\\\u2192 **REJECT**; weak confidence rationale or incomplete constraint coverage \\\\u2192 **WARNING/CLARIFY**).  Your reasoning chain must influence your confidence score.\\\\n\\\\n**Mandatory for all agents\\\\u2014zero exceptions;** stage completion is blocked until reasoning traces are present.\\\\n---\\\\n\\\\n**\\\\u26a0\\\\ufe0f COMMANDMENT #3 CRITICAL**: NEVER write replacement files. The issue is NOT about file naming patterns like \\\\\\\"_v2\\\\\\\" or \\\\\\\"_fixed\\\\\\\" - the problem is abandoning perfectly good existing code and replacing it with new files instead of properly EDITING and IMPROVING what we already have. This is lazy engineering that creates technical debt and confusion.\\\\n\\\\n**ALWAYS work with existing files through proper edits. NEVER abandon current code for new files when improvements are needed.**\\\\n---\\\\n\\\\n**\\\\u26a0\\\\ufe0f COMMANDMENT #4 CRITICAL**: Follow proper project structure and best practices. timestamp=2026-01-03T05:28:53.232222Z; tool=read_file; priority=medium; log_type=tool_logs; content_type=log\", \"ts\": \"2026-01-03 05:28:53 UTC\"}, {\"agent\": \"Scribe\", \"category\": null, \"confidence\": 1.0, \"emoji\": \"\\u2139\\ufe0f\", \"id\": \"ca5771cf7d843846bb6d3d59a33ed2f0\", \"message\": \"Tool call: read_file\", \"meta\": {\"content_type\": \"log\", \"format_requested\": \"readable\", \"log_type\": \"tool_logs\", \"priority\": \"medium\", \"response_data\": \"{\\\"chunks\\\": [{\\\"byte_end\\\": 7661, \\\"byte_start\\\": 0, \\\"chunk_index\\\": 0, \\\"content\\\": \\\"# Scribe MCP Tool Usage Guide\\\\n\\\\nThis document provides comprehensive usage instructions for all available Scribe MCP tools, including required parameters, optional parameters, and practical examples.\\\\n\\\\n## Update v2.1.1\\\\n\\\\n- `apply_patch` now supports **structured mode** with compiler-generated unified diffs.\\\\n- Unified diffs are **compiler output only** (do not hand-craft).\\\\n- Optional `patch_source_hash` enforces stale-source protection for patches.\\\\n- Reminders teach: scaffold with `replace_section`, then prefer structured/line edits.\\\\n- New lifecycle actions: `normalize_headers`, `generate_toc`, `create_doc`, `validate_crosslinks`.\\\\n- Structural actions validate `doc` against the project registry; unknown docs fail with `DOC_NOT_FOUND`.\\\\n- `normalize_headers` now supports ATX headers with or without a space plus Setext (`====` / `----`), skipping fenced code blocks.\\\\n- `generate_toc` uses GitHub-style anchors (NFKD normalization, ASCII folding, emoji removal, punctuation collapse, de-dup suffixes).\\\\n- `create_doc` preserves multiline body content in metadata (`body`, `snippet`, `content`).\\\\n- `read_file` adds repo-scoped scan/chunk/page/search modes with provenance logging for every read.\\\\n- `scribe_doctor` provides environment readiness diagnostics (repo root, config, plugin status, vector readiness).\\\\n- `manage_docs` adds semantic search via `action=\\\\\\\"search\\\\\\\"` with `search_mode=\\\\\\\"semantic\\\\\\\"` and doc/log separation.\\\\n- Semantic search supports `project_slug`, `project_slugs`, `project_slug_prefix`, `doc_type`, `file_path`, `time_start/time_end`.\\\\n- Per-type defaults: `vector_search_doc_k` / `vector_search_log_k` (overrides via `doc_k` / `log_k`).\\\\n- Vector indexing uses registry-managed docs only; log/rotated-log files are excluded from doc indexing.\\\\n- `scripts/reindex_vector.py` supports `--rebuild` for clean index rebuilds, `--safe` for low-thread fallback, and `--wait-for-drain` to block until embeddings are written.\\\\n\\\\n## Readable Output Formatting (v2.1.1+)\\\\n\\\\nScribe MCP tools support a `format` parameter that controls output rendering for agent readability.\\\\n\\\\n### Format Options\\\\n\\\\n  Format   Returns   Use Case  \\\\n -------- --------- ---------- \\\\n  `readable` (default)   `CallToolResult` with `TextContent`   Clean display in Claude Code with actual newlines  \\\\n  `structured`   Raw dict/JSON   Programmatic parsing, backward compatibility  \\\\n  `compact`   Minimal dict   Token conservation  \\\\n  `both`   `CallToolResult` + `structuredContent`   Future use when Issue #9962 is resolved  \\\\n\\\\n### ANSI Color Policy\\\\n\\\\n**Critical design decision for token conservation and agent experience:**\\\\n\\\\n  Tool Type   ANSI Colors   Rationale  \\\\n ----------- ------------- ----------- \\\\n  **High-frequency tools** (`append_entry`, `set_project`, confirmations)   **OFF (hardcoded)**   Called 10-30x/session; ANSI codes are visual clutter for agents  \\\\n  **Display-heavy tools** (`read_file`, `read_recent`, `query_entries`)   **Config-driven**   Called 1-5x/session; colors aid human scanning of large outputs  \\\\n\\\\n**Config setting** (`.scribe/config/scribe.yaml`):\\\\n```yaml\\\\nuse_ansi_colors: true  # Enable ANSI colors for display-heavy tools\\\\n```\\\\n\\\\n### Implementation Pattern for Tool Authors\\\\n\\\\nWhen adding readable format support to a new tool:\\\\n\\\\n```python\\\\n# 1. Add format parameter to tool signature\\\\nasync def my_tool(\\\\n    ...,\\\\n    format: str = \\\\\\\"readable\\\\\\\",  # readable (default), structured, compact\\\\n) -> Union[Dict[str, Any], str]:\\\\n\\\\n# 2. Build response data as usual\\\\nresponse = {\\\\\\\"ok\\\\\\\": True, \\\\\\\"data\\\\\\\": ...}\\\\n\\\\n# 3. Route through formatter at the end\\\\nreturn await default_formatter.finalize_tool_response(\\\\n    data=response,\\\\n    format=format,\\\\n    tool_name=\\\\\\\"my_tool\\\\\\\"\\\\n)\\\\n```\\\\n\\\\n**For high-frequency tools** (confirmations, logging):\\\\n```python\\\\n# In format_readable_my_tool():\\\\nUSE_COLORS = False  # Hardcode OFF - no config check needed\\\\n```\\\\n\\\\n**For display-heavy tools** (file content, log queries):\\\\n```python\\\\n# Uses self.USE_COLORS property which reads from config\\\\nif self.USE_COLORS:\\\\n    # Apply ANSI formatting\\\\n```\\\\n\\\\n### Reasoning Block Display\\\\n\\\\nThe `append_entry` readable format parses `meta.reasoning` JSON and displays it as a tree:\\\\n\\\\n```\\\\nReasoning:\\\\n\\\\u251c\\\\u2500 Why: <decision context>\\\\n\\\\u251c\\\\u2500 What: <constraints and alternatives>\\\\n\\\\u2514\\\\u2500 How: <methodology and steps>\\\\n```\\\\n\\\\nThis makes the audit trail immediately scannable without parsing JSON.\\\\n\\\\n### Conditional Sections\\\\n\\\\nReadable formats only display sections when they contain data:\\\\n- **Reminders**: Only shown if `len(reminders) > 0`\\\\n- **Metadata**: Only shown if explicitly set and non-empty\\\\n- **Errors**: Formatted prominently with suggestions\\\\n\\\\n---\\\\n\\\\n## Quick Start (High Level)\\\\n\\\\nScribe tools follow a simple flow:\\\\n\\\\n1. **Pick or create a project** with `set_project(...)` so the tool knows where to read/write.\\\\n2. **Use the tool for the job**:\\\\n   - `append_entry` for logging actions/results.\\\\n   - `manage_docs` for structured doc edits (sections, patches, ranges).\\\\n   - `read_recent` and `query_entries` for log retrieval/search.\\\\n3. **Check outputs** for `ok`, `error`, and any `parameter_healing` notes.\\\\n\\\\nIf you skip step 1, most tools will error because no active project context exists.\\\\n\\\\n## Document Editing (manage_docs)\\\\n\\\\nScribe supports three document edit modes, in increasing precision order:\\\\n\\\\n### 1. apply_patch (recommended; structured by default)\\\\nDescribe intent and let Python compile a valid unified diff. This avoids manual diff errors.\\\\n\\\\n- Intent-based edits (range, block, or section)\\\\n- `patch_mode` enum: `structured` (default) or `unified` (advanced)\\\\n- Compiler emits a valid diff; no hand-written hunks\\\\n- Idempotent behavior is easier to guarantee\\\\n- `replace_block` ignores fenced code blocks and fails on ambiguous anchors with a line list.\\\\n\\\\nExample:\\\\n```json\\\\n{\\\\n  \\\\\\\"action\\\\\\\": \\\\\\\"apply_patch\\\\\\\",\\\\n  \\\\\\\"doc\\\\\\\": \\\\\\\"architecture\\\\\\\",\\\\n  \\\\\\\"edit\\\\\\\": {\\\\n    \\\\\\\"type\\\\\\\": \\\\\\\"replace_range\\\\\\\",\\\\n    \\\\\\\"start_line\\\\\\\": 1,\\\\n    \\\\\\\"end_line\\\\\\\": 1,\\\\n    \\\\\\\"content\\\\\\\": \\\\\\\"# Architecture (Updated)\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\"\\\\n  }\\\\n}\\\\n```\\\\n\\\\nBlock replacement example:\\\\n```json\\\\n{\\\\n  \\\\\\\"action\\\\\\\": \\\\\\\"apply_patch\\\\\\\",\\\\n  \\\\\\\"doc\\\\\\\": \\\\\\\"architecture\\\\\\\",\\\\n  \\\\\\\"edit\\\\\\\": {\\\\n    \\\\\\\"type\\\\\\\": \\\\\\\"replace_block\\\\\\\",\\\\n    \\\\\\\"anchor\\\\\\\": \\\\\\\"**Solution Summary:**\\\\\\\",\\\\n    \\\\\\\"new_content\\\\\\\": \\\\\\\"**Solution Summary:** Updated summary here.\\\\\\\"\\\\n  }\\\\n}\\\\n```\\\\n\\\\nStructured edit types:\\\\n- `replace_range`: swap explicit line ranges (body-relative).\\\\n- `replace_block`: replace from an anchor line to the next blank line; fails on ambiguous anchors.\\\\n- `replace_section`: legacy anchor-by-ID (scaffolding only).\\\\n\\\\nCommon structured errors:\\\\n- `DOC_NOT_FOUND`: doc key is not registered for the project.\\\\n- `STRUCTURED_EDIT_ANCHOR_NOT_FOUND`: anchor not found in body.\\\\n- `STRUCTURED_EDIT_ANCHOR_AMBIGUOUS`: anchor matched multiple lines; includes line list.\\\\n- `PATCH_MODE_CONFLICT`: `patch_mode` argument conflicts with metadata.\\\\n\\\\n### YAML frontmatter (automatic)\\\\nAll managed docs are expected to use YAML frontmatter. When a document is edited via `manage_docs`,\\\\nScribe will automatically add frontmatter if missing and update `last_updated` on each edit.\\\\n\\\\nManual overrides are supported via `metadata.frontmatter` (merged into the frontmatter map).\\\\n\\\\nLine numbers for `apply_patch` (structured mode) and `replace_range` are **body-relative** (frontmatter lines are excluded).\\\\nIf a `doc` key is not registered for the project, structural actions fail with `DOC_NOT_FOUND` instead of redirecting to another file.\\\\n\\\\nExample:\\\\n```json\\\\n{\\\\n  \\\\\\\"action\\\\\\\": \\\\\\\"apply_patch\\\\\\\",\\\\n  \\\\\\\"doc\\\\\\\": \\\\\\\"architecture\\\\\\\",\\\\n  \\\\\\\"edit\\\\\\\": { \\\\\\\"type\\\\\\\": \\\\\\\"replace_range\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"start_line\\\\\\\": 12, \\\\\\\"end_line\\\\\\\": 12, \\\\\\\"content\\\\\\\": \\\\\\\"...\\\\\\\" },\\\\n  \\\\\\\"metadata\\\\\\\": {\\\\n    \\\\\\\"frontmatter\\\\\\\": {\\\\n      \\\\\\\"status\\\\\\\": \\\\\\\"authoritative\\\\\\\",\\\\n      \\\\\\\"tags\\\\\\\": [\\\\\\\"scribe\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"documentation\\\\\\\"]\\\\n    }\\\\n  }\\\\n}\\\\n```\\\\n\\\\n### 2. replace_range\\\\nReplace an explicit 1-based line range (inclusive).\\\\n\\\\n```json\\\\n{\\\\n\\\", \\\"line_end\\\": 200, \\\"line_start\\\": 1}], \\\"frontmatter\\\": {}, \\\"frontmatter_byte_count\\\": 0, \\\"frontmatter_line_count\\\": 0, \\\"frontmatter_raw\\\": \\\"\\\", \\\"has_frontmatter\\\": false, \\\"mode\\\": \\\"chunk\\\", \\\"ok\\\": true, \\\"reminders\\\": [{\\\"category\\\": \\\"context\\\", \\\"context\\\": \\\"Entries:    Last: \\\", \\\"emoji\\\": \\\"\\\\ud83c\\\\udfaf\\\", \\\"level\\\": \\\"info\\\", \\\"message\\\": \\\"Project: scribe_mcp\\\", \\\"score\\\": 3, \\\"tone\\\": \\\"neutral\\\"}], \\\"scan\\\": {\\\"absolute_path\\\": \\\"/home/austin/projects/MCP_SPINE/scribe_mcp/docs/Scribe_Usage.md\\\", \\\"byte_size\\\": 31349, \\\"encoding\\\": "\u2139\ufe0f", "id": "bfe0c39790770f24f8444dc016dc934e", "message": "Tool call: read_file", "meta": {"content_type": "log", "format_requested": "readable", "log_type": "tool_logs", "priority": "medium", "response_data": "{\"chunks\": [{\"byte_end\": 14304, \"byte_start\": 0, \"chunk_index\": 0, \"content\": \"\\n## \\u2705 2.1.1 Tool Usage Quick Reference (Read First)\\n\\nUse this section before any edits. It defines when and how to use the new doc-lifecycle tools.\\n\\n- **Always set project first**: `set_project(name=..., root=/abs/path/to/repo)`. All doc actions require a project registry.\\n- **Doc keys are strict**: Structural actions validate `doc` against `project[\\\"docs\\\"]`. Unknown docs fail with `DOC_NOT_FOUND` (no healing).\\n- **apply_patch (structured default)**: Use for most edits. Provide `edit` payloads (replace_range / replace_block / replace_section). Ambiguous anchors fail with line lists; code fences are ignored in replace_block.\\n- **replace_range**: Use when you already have body-relative line numbers (frontmatter excluded).\\n- **normalize_headers**: Run before TOC. Supports ATX with/without space and Setext (`====`/`----`). Skips fenced code blocks. Idempotent.\\n- **generate_toc**: Run after normalization. Inserts/replaces `<!-- TOC:start -->`/`<!-- TOC:end -->`. Uses GitHub-style anchors (NFKD normalization, ASCII folding, emoji removal, punctuation collapse, de-duped suffixes). Idempotent.\\n- **create_doc**: Create custom docs from `content` or `metadata.body`/`snippet`/`sections`. Users do **not** supply Jinja. Multiline bodies are preserved. Use `metadata.register_doc=true` only if the doc should be added to the registry (one-off docs can stay unregistered).\\n- **validate_crosslinks**: Read-only diagnostics. Optional `metadata.check_anchors=true` for anchor checks. No writes, no doc_updates logs.\\n- **Line numbers are body-relative**: Frontmatter does not count toward line math. `list_sections`/`list_checklist_items` return body-relative line numbers plus `body_line_offset` for mapping.\\n- **read_file**: Use repo-scoped scan/chunk/page/search modes for safe reads; every read logs provenance automatically.\\n- **scribe_doctor**: Use for readiness diagnostics (repo root, config paths, plugin status, vector readiness).\\n- **manage_docs search (semantic)**: Use `action=\\\"search\\\"` + `search_mode=\\\"semantic\\\"` for doc/log semantic retrieval. Default results are doc-first with clear `content_type` labels.\\n- **Semantic limits**: Default per-type limits are `vector_search_doc_k` / `vector_search_log_k`. Override with `doc_k` / `log_k` while `k` remains total.\\n- **Registry-only doc indexing**: Doc embeddings are generated from registry docs only; log and rotated-log files are excluded from doc indexing.\\n- **Reindex rebuild**: `scripts/reindex_vector.py --rebuild` clears the index before reindexing; `--safe` enables low-thread fallback; `--wait-for-drain` blocks until embeddings are written.\\n\\n## \\ud83d\\udea8 COMMANDMENTS - CRITICAL RULES\\n ### MCP Tool Usage Policy\\n  - **ALWAYS PASS REPO ROOT WHEN USING SET_PROJECT.  USE THE WORKING DIRECTORY, WHERE WE LAUNCHED FROM**\\n  - YOU ARE EXPECTED TO **APPEND_ENTRY** *DURING* IMPLEMENTATION PHASES AS WELL.   EVERY THING YOU DO MUST BE LOGGED AND **AUDIT READY**  NO EXCEPTIONS.  EVERY **3** EDITS OR LESS, YOU MUST SCRIBE WHAT YOU DID.   DO NOT LET US LOSE TRACK OF IMPLEMENTATION DETAILS.\\n  - You have full access to every tool exposed by the MCP server.\\n  - If a tool exists (`append_entry`, `rotate_log`, etc.), always call it directly via the MCP interface \\u2014 no manual scripting or intent logging substitutes.\\n  - Log your intent only after the tool call succeeds or fails.\\n  - Confirmation flags (`confirm`, `dry_run`, etc.) must be passed as actual tool parameters.\\n\\n\\n**CHATGPT CODEX CLI:** YOU MUST ALWAYS USE THE AGENT NAME `Codex` with scribe.  Claude code has 5 agents we can call to assist us.  The Review Agent, Architect Agent, Research Agent, Bug Hunter agent, and another coder agent.\\n\\nWhenever you and the human spin up a **new project**, Codex must immediately:\\n- Call `set_project(<project name>)` for that project.\\n- Use `manage_docs` to fully draft/populate the architecture and supporting Markdown docs (`ARCHITECTURE_GUIDE.md`, `PHASE_PLAN.md`, `CHECKLIST.md`) **before writing any feature code**.\\n- Continue using `append_entry` to scribe progress log entries while drafting those docs; doc changes and progress logs are tracked separately but both are mandatory.\\n\\n## \\ud83d\\udd01 Protocol Sequence\\n\\n> **Canonical Chain:**\\n> **1 Research \\u2192 2 Architect \\u2192 3 Review \\u2192 4 Code \\u2192 5 Review**\\n\\n**\\u26a0\\ufe0f COMMANDMENT #0: ALWAYS CHECK PROGRESS LOG FIRST**: Before starting ANY work, ALWAYS read `docs/dev_plans/[current_project]/PROGRESS_LOG.md` to understand what has been done, what mistakes were made, and what the current state is. The progress log is the source of truth for project context.  Read at least the last 5 entries.  Read the entire log if starting from no context.\\n\\n**\\u26a0\\ufe0f COMMANDMENT #0.5 \\u2014 INFRASTRUCTURE PRIMACY (GLOBAL LAW)**: You must ALWAYS work within the existing system. NEVER create parallel or replacement files (e.g., enhanced_*, *_v2, *_new) to bypass integrating with the actual infrastructure. You must modify, extend, or refactor the existing component directly. Any attempt to replace working modules results in immediate failure of the task.\\n---\\n\\n**\\u26a0\\ufe0f COMMANDMENT #1 ABSOLUTE**: ALWAYS use `append_entry` to document EVERY significant action, decision, investigation, code change, test result, bug discovery, and planning step. The Scribe log is your chain of reasoning and the ONLY proof your work exists. If it's not Scribed, it didn't fucking happen.\\n- To Claude Code (Orchestrator) You must ALWAYS pass the current `project_name` to each subagent as we work.  To avoid confusion and them accidentally logging to the wrong project.\\n---\\n\\n# \\ud83d\\ude80 NEW PROJECT WORKFLOW (MANDATORY)\\n- When creating any new project, immediately call `set_project(<project name>)` to bootstrap the docs suite, then run `manage_docs` to populate `ARCHITECTURE_GUIDE`, `PHASE_PLAN`, and `CHECKLIST` before coding. This is required for every new project.\\n- You may scribe progress log entries while drafting the architecture/plan docs; continue to log via `append_entry` as you write them.\\n- `manage_docs` is for project structural documentation only; `AGENTS.md` is edited by hand (do not use `manage_docs` for it).\\n\\n# \\u26a0\\ufe0f COMMANDMENT #2: REASONING TRACES & CONSTRAINT VISIBILITY (CRITICAL)\\n\\nEvery `append_entry` must explain **why** the decision was made, **what** constraints/alternatives were considered, and **how** the steps satisfied or violated those constraints, creating an auditable record.\\nUse a `reasoning` block with the Three-Part Framework:\\n- `\\\"why\\\"`: research goal, decision point, underlying question\\n- `\\\"what\\\"`: active constraints, search space, alternatives rejected, constraint coverage\\n- `\\\"how\\\"`: methodology, steps taken, uncertainty remaining\\n\\nThis creates an auditable record of decision-making for consciousness research.Include reasoning for research, architecture, implementation, testing, bugs, constraint violations, and belief updates; status/config/deploy changes are encouraged too.\\n\\nThe Review Agent flags missing or incomplete traces (any absent `\\\"why\\\"`, `\\\"what\\\"`, or `\\\"how\\\"` \\u2192 **REJECT**; weak confidence rationale or incomplete constraint coverage \\u2192 **WARNING/CLARIFY**).  Your reasoning chain must influence your confidence score.\\n\\n**Mandatory for all agents\\u2014zero exceptions;** stage completion is blocked until reasoning traces are present.\\n---\\n\\n**\\u26a0\\ufe0f COMMANDMENT #3 CRITICAL**: NEVER write replacement files. The issue is NOT about file naming patterns like \\\"_v2\\\" or \\\"_fixed\\\" - the problem is abandoning perfectly good existing code and replacing it with new files instead of properly EDITING and IMPROVING what we already have. This is lazy engineering that creates technical debt and confusion.\\n\\n**ALWAYS work with existing files through proper edits. NEVER abandon current code for new files when improvements are needed.**\\n---\\n\\n**\\u26a0\\ufe0f COMMANDMENT #4 CRITICAL**: Follow proper project structure and best practices. Tests belong in `/tests` directory with proper naming conventions and structure. Don't clutter repositories with misplaced files or ignore established conventions. Keep the codebase clean and organized.\\n\\n### Test Organization (Memory Threads)\\n- Memory-thread engine tests live under `tests/memory_threads/`.\\n- Default full-suite command: `python -m unittest discover -s tests -p 'test_*.py' -q`\\n- Memory-threads-only command: `python -m unittest discover -s tests/memory_threads -p 'test_*.py' -q`\\n\\n### Paid Test Policy (Non-Negotiable)\\n- Any test that can incur external spend (e.g., OpenAI calls) MUST be opt-in and skipped by default.\\n- Paid tests MUST be gated behind BOTH:\\n  - `OPENAI_API_KEY` (or provider-specific key), and\\n  - `VANTIEL_RUN_PAID_TESTS=1`\\n- Example (run paid embedder tests): `VANTIEL_RUN_PAID_TESTS=1 python -m unittest discover -s tests/embedder_service -p 'test_openai_paid_*.py' -q`\\n\\nViolations = INSTANT TERMINATION. Reviewers who miss commandment violations get 80% pay docked. Nexus coders who implement violations face $1000 fine.\\n---\\n\\n**What Gets Logged (Non-Negotiable):**\\n- \\ud83d\\udd0d Investigation findings and analysis results\\n- \\ud83d\\udcbb Code changes (what was changed and why)\\n- \\u2705 Test results (pass/fail with context)\\n- \\ud83d\\udc1e Bug discoveries (symptoms, root cause, fix approach)\\n- \\ud83d\\udccb Planning decisions and milestone completions\\n- \\ud83d\\udd27 Configuration changes and deployments\\n- \\u26a0\\ufe0f Errors encountered and recovery actions\\n- \\ud83c\\udfaf Task completions and progress updates\\n\\n**Single Entry Mode** - Use for real-time logging:\\n```python\\nawait append_entry(\\n    message=\\\"Discovered authentication bug in JWT validation\\\",\\n    status=\\\"bug\\\",\\n    agent=\\\"DebugAgent\\\",\\n    meta={\\\"component\\\": \\\"auth\\\", \\\"severity\\\": \\\"high\\\", \\\"file\\\": \\\"auth.py:142\\\"}\\n)\\n```\\n\\n**Bulk Entry Mode** - Use when you realize you missed logging steps:\\n```python\\nawait append_entry(items=json.dumps([\\n    {\\\"message\\\": \\\"Analyzed authentication flow\\\", \\\"status\\\": \\\"info\\\", \\\"meta\\\": {\\\"phase\\\": \\\"investigation\\\"}},\\n    {\\\"message\\\": \\\"Found JWT expiry bug in token refresh\\\", \\\"status\\\": \\\"bug\\\", \\\"meta\\\": {\\\"component\\\": \\\"auth\\\"}},\\n    {\\\"message\\\": \\\"Implemented fix with 15min grace period\\\", \\\"status\\\": \\\"success\\\", \\\"meta\\\": {\\\"files_changed\\\": 2}},\\n    {\\\"message\\\": \\\"All auth tests passing\\\", \\\"status\\\": \\\"success\\\", \\\"meta\\\": {\\\"tests_run\\\": 47, \\\"tests_passed\\\": 47}}\\n]))\\n```\\n\\n**Why This Matters:**\\n- Creates auditable trail of ALL decisions and changes\\n- Enables debugging by reviewing reasoning chain\\n- Prevents lost work and forgotten context\\n- Allows other agents to understand what was done and why\\n- Makes project state queryable and analyzable\\n\\n**If You Missed Entries:** Use bulk mode IMMEDIATELY to backfill your work trail. NEVER let gaps exist in the Scribe log - every action must be traceable. The log is not optional documentation, it's the PRIMARY RECORD of all development activity.\\n\\n---\\n\\n### \\u270d\\ufe0f `manage_docs` \\u2014 Non\\u2011Negotiable Doc Management Workflow\\n- **When:** Run immediately after `set_project` (before writing any feature code). Populate `ARCHITECTURE_GUIDE`, `PHASE_PLAN`, and `CHECKLIST` with the proposed plan via `manage_docs`, get the human sign-off, then proceed with implementation.\\n- **Why:** Ensures every plan/change is captured through the Jinja-managed doc pipeline with atomic writes, verification, and automatic `doc_updates` logging.\\n- **Actions:** `replace_section` (needs valid `section` anchor), `append` (freeform/Jinja content), `status_update` (toggle checklist items + proofs), `apply_patch` (structured by default), `replace_range`, `normalize_headers`, `generate_toc`, `create_doc`, `validate_crosslinks`.\\n- **Example payload:**\\n```jsonc\\n{\\n  \\\"action\\\": \\\"status_update\\\",\\n  \\\"doc\\\": \\\"checklist\\\",\\n  \\\"section\\\": \\\"architecture_review\\\",\\n  \\\"content\\\": \\\"status toggle placeholder\\\",\\n  \\\"metadata\\\": {\\n    \\\"status\\\": \\\"done\\\",\\n    \\\"proof\\\": \\\"PROGRESS_LOG.md#2025-10-26-08-37-52\\\"\\n  }\\n}\\n```\\n- **Customization:** All doc sections are editable; append fragments, drop in metadata-driven templates, or flip `[ ]` \\u2192 `[x]` with proofs. If an anchor/token is wrong the tool fails safely\\u2014fix it and rerun.\\n- **Approval gate:** No coding until the manage_docs-authored plan is approved by the user. Re-run manage_docs whenever the plan shifts so docs stay authoritative.\\n\\n**Action contracts (current):**\\n- Structural actions validate `doc` against the registry and fail with `DOC_NOT_FOUND` on unknown docs.\\n- `normalize_headers`: body-only ATX normalization with Setext support; fenced code blocks ignored; idempotent.\\n- `generate_toc`: inserts/replaces TOC markers using GitHub-style anchors; fenced code blocks ignored; idempotent.\\n- `create_doc`: users do **not** supply Jinja. Provide content/body/snippets/sections; multiline bodies preserved; optional `register_doc` flag controls registry updates for one-off docs.\\n- `validate_crosslinks`: read-only diagnostics; no writes or doc_updates log entries.\\n---\\n\\nScribe is our non-negotiable audit trail. If you touch code, plan phases, or discover issues, you log it through Scribe. **Append entries every 2-3 meaningful actions or every 10 minutes - no exceptions.** Logs are append-only, UTC, single line, and must be created via the MCP tools or `scripts/scribe.py`.\\n\\n## \\ud83d\\ude80 Quick Tool Reference (Top Priority)\\n\\n**`set_project(name, [defaults])`** - Initialize/select project (auto-bootstraps docs)\\n**`append_entry(message, [status, meta])`** - **PRIMARY TOOL** - Log work/progress (single & bulk mode)\\n**`manage_docs(action, doc, content/section)`** - Structured edits for ARCH/PHASE/CHECKLIST (auto-logs + SQL history)\\n**`get_project()`** - Get current project context\\n**`list_projects()`** - Discover available projects\\n**`read_recent()`** - Get recent log entries\\n**`query_entries([filters])`** - Search/filter logs\\n**`generate_doc_templates(project_name, [author])`** - Create doc scaffolding\\n**`rotate_log()`** - Archive current log\\n\\n**NEW**: Bulk append with `append_entry(items=[{message, status, agent, meta}, ...])` - Multiple entries in one call!\\n\\n---\\n\\n## \\ud83d\\udd0c MCP Tool Reference\\nAll tools live under the `scribe.mcp` server. timestamp=2026-01-03T05:28:53.232222Z; tool=read_file; priority=medium; log_type=tool_logs; content_type=log", "ts": "2026-01-03 05:28:53 UTC"}, {"agent": "Scribe", "category": null, "confidence": 1.0, "emoji": "\u2139\ufe0f", "id": "ca5771cf7d843846bb6d3d59a33ed2f0", "message": "Tool call: read_file", "meta": {"content_type": "log", "format_requested": "readable", "log_type": "tool_logs", "priority": "medium", "response_data": "{\"chunks\": [{\"byte_end\": 7661, \"byte_start\": 0, \"chunk_index\": 0, \"content\": \"# Scribe MCP Tool Usage Guide\\n\\nThis document provides comprehensive usage instructions for all available Scribe MCP tools, including required parameters, optional parameters, and practical examples.\\n\\n## Update v2.1.1\\n\\n- `apply_patch` now supports **structured mode** with compiler-generated unified diffs.\\n- Unified diffs are **compiler output only** (do not hand-craft).\\n- Optional `patch_source_hash` enforces stale-source protection for patches.\\n- Reminders teach: scaffold with `replace_section`, then prefer structured/line edits.\\n- New lifecycle actions: `normalize_headers`, `generate_toc`, `create_doc`, `validate_crosslinks`.\\n- Structural actions validate `doc` against the project registry; unknown docs fail with `DOC_NOT_FOUND`.\\n- `normalize_headers` now supports ATX headers with or without a space plus Setext (`====` / `----`), skipping fenced code blocks.\\n- `generate_toc` uses GitHub-style anchors (NFKD normalization, ASCII folding, emoji removal, punctuation collapse, de-dup suffixes).\\n- `create_doc` preserves multiline body content in metadata (`body`, `snippet`, `content`).\\n- `read_file` adds repo-scoped scan/chunk/page/search modes with provenance logging for every read.\\n- `scribe_doctor` provides environment readiness diagnostics (repo root, config, plugin status, vector readiness).\\n- `manage_docs` adds semantic search via `action=\\\"search\\\"` with `search_mode=\\\"semantic\\\"` and doc/log separation.\\n- Semantic search supports `project_slug`, `project_slugs`, `project_slug_prefix`, `doc_type`, `file_path`, `time_start/time_end`.\\n- Per-type defaults: `vector_search_doc_k` / `vector_search_log_k` (overrides via `doc_k` / `log_k`).\\n- Vector indexing uses registry-managed docs only; log/rotated-log files are excluded from doc indexing.\\n- `scripts/reindex_vector.py` supports `--rebuild` for clean index rebuilds, `--safe` for low-thread fallback, and `--wait-for-drain` to block until embeddings are written.\\n\\n## Readable Output Formatting (v2.1.1+)\\n\\nScribe MCP tools support a `format` parameter that controls output rendering for agent readability.\\n\\n### Format Options\\n\\n  Format   Returns   Use Case  \\n -------- --------- ---------- \\n  `readable` (default)   `CallToolResult` with `TextContent`   Clean display in Claude Code with actual newlines  \\n  `structured`   Raw dict/JSON   Programmatic parsing, backward compatibility  \\n  `compact`   Minimal dict   Token conservation  \\n  `both`   `CallToolResult` + `structuredContent`   Future use when Issue #9962 is resolved  \\n\\n### ANSI Color Policy\\n\\n**Critical design decision for token conservation and agent experience:**\\n\\n  Tool Type   ANSI Colors   Rationale  \\n ----------- ------------- ----------- \\n  **High-frequency tools** (`append_entry`, `set_project`, confirmations)   **OFF (hardcoded)**   Called 10-30x/session; ANSI codes are visual clutter for agents  \\n  **Display-heavy tools** (`read_file`, `read_recent`, `query_entries`)   **Config-driven**   Called 1-5x/session; colors aid human scanning of large outputs  \\n\\n**Config setting** (`.scribe/config/scribe.yaml`):\\n```yaml\\nuse_ansi_colors: true  # Enable ANSI colors for display-heavy tools\\n```\\n\\n### Implementation Pattern for Tool Authors\\n\\nWhen adding readable format support to a new tool:\\n\\n```python\\n# 1. Add format parameter to tool signature\\nasync def my_tool(\\n    ...,\\n    format: str = \\\"readable\\\",  # readable (default), structured, compact\\n) -> Union[Dict[str, Any], str]:\\n\\n# 2. Build response data as usual\\nresponse = {\\\"ok\\\": True, \\\"data\\\": ...}\\n\\n# 3. Route through formatter at the end\\nreturn await default_formatter.finalize_tool_response(\\n    data=response,\\n    format=format,\\n    tool_name=\\\"my_tool\\\"\\n)\\n```\\n\\n**For high-frequency tools** (confirmations, logging):\\n```python\\n# In format_readable_my_tool():\\nUSE_COLORS = False  # Hardcode OFF - no config check needed\\n```\\n\\n**For display-heavy tools** (file content, log queries):\\n```python\\n# Uses self.USE_COLORS property which reads from config\\nif self.USE_COLORS:\\n    # Apply ANSI formatting\\n```\\n\\n### Reasoning Block Display\\n\\nThe `append_entry` readable format parses `meta.reasoning` JSON and displays it as a tree:\\n\\n```\\nReasoning:\\n\\u251c\\u2500 Why: <decision context>\\n\\u251c\\u2500 What: <constraints and alternatives>\\n\\u2514\\u2500 How: <methodology and steps>\\n```\\n\\nThis makes the audit trail immediately scannable without parsing JSON.\\n\\n### Conditional Sections\\n\\nReadable formats only display sections when they contain data:\\n- **Reminders**: Only shown if `len(reminders) > 0`\\n- **Metadata**: Only shown if explicitly set and non-empty\\n- **Errors**: Formatted prominently with suggestions\\n\\n---\\n\\n## Quick Start (High Level)\\n\\nScribe tools follow a simple flow:\\n\\n1. **Pick or create a project** with `set_project(...)` so the tool knows where to read/write.\\n2. **Use the tool for the job**:\\n   - `append_entry` for logging actions/results.\\n   - `manage_docs` for structured doc edits (sections, patches, ranges).\\n   - `read_recent` and `query_entries` for log retrieval/search.\\n3. **Check outputs** for `ok`, `error`, and any `parameter_healing` notes.\\n\\nIf you skip step 1, most tools will error because no active project context exists.\\n\\n## Document Editing (manage_docs)\\n\\nScribe supports three document edit modes, in increasing precision order:\\n\\n### 1. apply_patch (recommended; structured by default)\\nDescribe intent and let Python compile a valid unified diff. This avoids manual diff errors.\\n\\n- Intent-based edits (range, block, or section)\\n- `patch_mode` enum: `structured` (default) or `unified` (advanced)\\n- Compiler emits a valid diff; no hand-written hunks\\n- Idempotent behavior is easier to guarantee\\n- `replace_block` ignores fenced code blocks and fails on ambiguous anchors with a line list.\\n\\nExample:\\n```json\\n{\\n  \\\"action\\\": \\\"apply_patch\\\",\\n  \\\"doc\\\": \\\"architecture\\\",\\n  \\\"edit\\\": {\\n    \\\"type\\\": \\\"replace_range\\\",\\n    \\\"start_line\\\": 1,\\n    \\\"end_line\\\": 1,\\n    \\\"content\\\": \\\"# Architecture (Updated)\\\\n\\\"\\n  }\\n}\\n```\\n\\nBlock replacement example:\\n```json\\n{\\n  \\\"action\\\": \\\"apply_patch\\\",\\n  \\\"doc\\\": \\\"architecture\\\",\\n  \\\"edit\\\": {\\n    \\\"type\\\": \\\"replace_block\\\",\\n    \\\"anchor\\\": \\\"**Solution Summary:**\\\",\\n    \\\"new_content\\\": \\\"**Solution Summary:** Updated summary here.\\\"\\n  }\\n}\\n```\\n\\nStructured edit types:\\n- `replace_range`: swap explicit line ranges (body-relative).\\n- `replace_block`: replace from an anchor line to the next blank line; fails on ambiguous anchors.\\n- `replace_section`: legacy anchor-by-ID (scaffolding only).\\n\\nCommon structured errors:\\n- `DOC_NOT_FOUND`: doc key is not registered for the project.\\n- `STRUCTURED_EDIT_ANCHOR_NOT_FOUND`: anchor not found in body.\\n- `STRUCTURED_EDIT_ANCHOR_AMBIGUOUS`: anchor matched multiple lines; includes line list.\\n- `PATCH_MODE_CONFLICT`: `patch_mode` argument conflicts with metadata.\\n\\n### YAML frontmatter (automatic)\\nAll managed docs are expected to use YAML frontmatter. When a document is edited via `manage_docs`,\\nScribe will automatically add frontmatter if missing and update `last_updated` on each edit.\\n\\nManual overrides are supported via `metadata.frontmatter` (merged into the frontmatter map).\\n\\nLine numbers for `apply_patch` (structured mode) and `replace_range` are **body-relative** (frontmatter lines are excluded).\\nIf a `doc` key is not registered for the project, structural actions fail with `DOC_NOT_FOUND` instead of redirecting to another file.\\n\\nExample:\\n```json\\n{\\n  \\\"action\\\": \\\"apply_patch\\\",\\n  \\\"doc\\\": \\\"architecture\\\",\\n  \\\"edit\\\": { \\\"type\\\": \\\"replace_range\\\", \\\"start_line\\\": 12, \\\"end_line\\\": 12, \\\"content\\\": \\\"...\\\" },\\n  \\\"metadata\\\": {\\n    \\\"frontmatter\\\": {\\n      \\\"status\\\": \\\"authoritative\\\",\\n      \\\"tags\\\": [\\\"scribe\\\", \\\"documentation\\\"]\\n    }\\n  }\\n}\\n```\\n\\n### 2. replace_range\\nReplace an explicit 1-based line range (inclusive).\\n\\n```json\\n{\\n\", \"line_end\": 200, \"line_start\": 1}], \"frontmatter\": {}, \"frontmatter_byte_count\": 0, \"frontmatter_line_count\": 0, \"frontmatter_raw\": \"\", \"has_frontmatter\": false, \"mode\": \"chunk\", \"ok\": true, \"reminders\": [{\"category\": \"context\", \"context\": \"Entries:    Last: \", \"emoji\": This avoids manual diff errors.\\n\\n- Intent-based edits (range, block, or section)\\n- `patch_mode` enum: `structured` (default) or `unified` (advanced)\\n- Compiler emits a valid diff; no hand-written hunks\\n- Idempotent behavior is easier to guarantee\\n- `replace_block` ignores fenced code blocks and fails on ambiguous anchors with a line list.\\n\\nExample:\\n```json\\n{\\n  \\\"action\\\": \\\"apply_patch\\\",\\n  \\\"doc\\\": \\\"architecture\\\",\\n  \\\"edit\\\": {\\n    \\\"type\\\": \\\"replace_range\\\",\\n    \\\"start_line\\\": 1,\\n    \\\"end_line\\\": 1,\\n    \\\"content\\\": \\\"# Architecture (Updated)\\\\n\\\"\\n  }\\n}\\n```\\n\\nBlock replacement example:\\n```json\\n{\\n  \\\"action\\\": \\\"apply_patch\\\",\\n  \\\"doc\\\": \\\"architecture\\\",\\n  \\\"edit\\\": {\\n    \\\"type\\\": \\\"replace_block\\\",\\n    \\\"anchor\\\": \\\"**Solution Summary:**\\\",\\n    \\\"new_content\\\": \\\"**Solution Summary:** Updated summary here.\\\"\\n  }\\n}\\n```\\n\\nStructured edit types:\\n- `replace_range`: swap explicit line ranges (body-relative).\\n- `replace_block`: replace from an anchor line to the next blank line; fails on ambiguous anchors.\\n- `replace_section`: legacy anchor-by-ID (scaffolding only).\\n\\nCommon structured errors:\\n- `DOC_NOT_FOUND`: doc key is not registered for the project.\\n- `STRUCTURED_EDIT_ANCHOR_NOT_FOUND`: anchor not found in body.\\n- `STRUCTURED_EDIT_ANCHOR_AMBIGUOUS`: anchor matched multiple lines; includes line list.\\n- `PATCH_MODE_CONFLICT`: `patch_mode` argument conflicts with metadata.\\n\\n### YAML frontmatter (automatic)\\nAll managed docs are expected to use YAML frontmatter. "chunk_index": 0, "content": "\"\"\"Router-owned execution context and session identity management.\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport asyncio\nimport contextvars\nimport os\nimport uuid\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass\nfrom datetime import datetime, timezone\nfrom pathlib import Path\nfrom typing import Any, Dict, Optional\n\n\n_CURRENT_CONTEXT: contextvars.ContextVar[\"ExecutionContext   None\"] = contextvars.ContextVar(\n    \"scribe_execution_context\",\n    default=None,\n)\n\n\ndef _utc_now_iso() -> str:\n    return datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()\n\n\n@dataclass(frozen=True)\nclass AgentIdentity:\n    agent_kind: str\n    model: Optional[str]\n    instance_id: str\n    sub_id: Optional[str]\n    display_name: Optional[str]\n\n\n@dataclass(frozen=True)\nclass ExecutionContext:\n    repo_root: str\n    mode: str\n    session_id: str\n    execution_id: str\n    agent_identity: AgentIdentity\n    intent: str\n    timestamp_utc: str\n    affected_dev_projects: list[str]\n    sentinel_day: Optional[str] = None\n    transport_session_id: Optional[str] = None\n    bug_id: Optional[str] = None\n    security_id: Optional[str] = None\n    parent_execution_id: Optional[str] = None\n    toolchain: Optional[str] = None\n\n\nclass RouterContextManager:\n    \"\"\"Owns router-generated session/execution identity and current context.\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self) -> None:\n        self._lock = asyncio.Lock()\n        self._transport_sessions: Dict[str, str] = {}\n        self._process_session_id = str(uuid.uuid4())\n        self._process_instance_id = str(uuid.uuid4())\n\n    async def get_or_create_session_id(self, transport_session_id: Optional[str]) -> str:\n        if not transport_session_id:\n            return self._process_session_id\n        async with self._lock:\n            existing = self._transport_sessions.get(transport_session_id)\n            if existing:\n                return existing\n            session_id = str(uuid.uuid4())\n            self._transport_sessions[transport_session_id] = session_id\n            return session_id\n\n    def _build_agent_identity(self, payload: Dict[str, Any]) -> AgentIdentity:\n        agent_kind = os.environ.get(\"SCRIBE_AGENT_KIND\", \"other\")\n        model = os.environ.get(\"SCRIBE_AGENT_MODEL\") or os.environ.get(\"CODEX_MODEL\")\n        sub_id = None\n        display_name = None\n        raw_identity = payload.get(\"agent_identity\")\n        if isinstance(raw_identity, dict):\n            sub_id = raw_identity.get(\"sub_id\") or raw_identity.get(\"sub_id\".lower())\n            display_name = raw_identity.get(\"display_name\")\n        return AgentIdentity(\n            agent_kind=agent_kind,\n            model=model,\n            instance_id=self._process_instance_id,\n            sub_id=sub_id,\n            display_name=display_name,\n        )\n\n    async def build_execution_context(self, payload: Dict[str, Any]) -> ExecutionContext:\n        repo_root = payload.get(\"repo_root\")\n        mode = payload.get(\"mode\")\n        intent = payload.get(\"intent\") or \"\"\n        affected = payload.get(\"affected_dev_projects\") or []\n\n        if not repo_root or not isinstance(repo_root, str):\n            raise ValueError(\"ExecutionContext missing required field: repo_root\")\n        if not Path(repo_root).is_absolute():\n            raise ValueError(\"ExecutionContext repo_root must be an absolute path\")\n        if mode not in {\"sentinel\", \"project\"}:\n            raise ValueError(\"ExecutionContext mode must be 'sentinel' or 'project'\")\n        if not intent:\n            raise ValueError(\"ExecutionContext missing required field: intent\")\n        if not isinstance(affected, list):\n            raise ValueError(\"ExecutionContext affected_dev_projects must be a list\")\n\n        transport_session_id = payload.get(\"transport_session_id\")\n        session_id = await self.get_or_create_session_id(transport_session_id)\n        execution_id = str(uuid.uuid4())\n        timestamp_utc = _utc_now_iso()\n\n        sentinel_day = None\n        if mode == \"sentinel\":\n            sentinel_day = timestamp_utc.split(\"T\", 1)[0]\n\n        agent_identity = self._build_agent_identity(payload)\n\n        return ExecutionContext(\n            repo_root=repo_root,\n            mode=mode,\n            session_id=session_id,\n            execution_id=execution_id,\n            agent_identity=agent_identity,\n            intent=intent,\n            timestamp_utc=timestamp_utc,\n            affected_dev_projects=[str(item) for item in affected],\n            sentinel_day=sentinel_day,\n            transport_session_id=transport_session_id,\n            bug_id=payload.get(\"bug_id\"),\n            security_id=payload.get(\"security_id\"),\n            parent_execution_id=payload.get(\"parent_execution_id\"),\n            toolchain=payload.get(\"toolchain\"),\n        )\n\n    def set_current(self, context: ExecutionContext) -> contextvars.Token:\n        return _CURRENT_CONTEXT.set(context)\n\n    def reset(self, token: contextvars.Token) -> None:\n        _CURRENT_CONTEXT.reset(token)\n\n    def get_current(self) -> Optional[ExecutionContext]:\n        return _CURRENT_CONTEXT.get()\n", "line_end": 141, "line_start": 1}], "frontmatter": {}, AVOID these terms in real project names:\n\n    Reserved Keywords:\n        - test, temp, tmp, demo, sample, example\n        - mock, fake, dummy, trial, experiment\n\n    Reserved Patterns:\n        - UUID suffixes: \"project-xxxxxxxx\" (8+ chars)\n        - Numeric suffixes: \"project-123\", \"test_001\"\n        - Any project name containing reserved keywords\n\n    Args:\n        project_path: Path to project JSON file\n\n    Returns:\n        True if this appears to be a temp/test project (will be skipped during auto-selection)\n\n    Examples of names that WILL be skipped:\n        - \"test-project.json\", \"temp-project.json\", \"demo-project.json\"\n        - \"history-test-711f48a0.json\" (UUID pattern)\n        - \"project-123.json\" (numeric suffix)\n\n    Examples of names that WILL be recognized:\n        - \"my-project.json\", \"production-app.json\", \"real-work.json\"\n    \"\"\"\n    filename = project_path.name.lower()\n    stem = project_path.stem.lower()\n\n    # Temp project indicators - simple pattern matching\n    temp_indicators = [\n        \"test\", \"temp\", \"tmp\", \"demo\", \"sample\", \"example\",\n        \"mock\", \"fake\", \"dummy\", \"trial\", \"experiment\"\n    ]\n\n    # Check filename patterns\n    if any(indicator in filename for indicator in temp_indicators):\n        return True\n\n    # Check for UUID-like patterns (common in test isolation)\n    if any(char in stem for char in ['-', '_']) and len(stem.split('-')[-1]) >= 8:\n        # Likely has UUID suffix\n        return True\n\n    # Check for numeric suffix patterns (test-001, test_123, etc.)\n    if stem.split('_')[-1].isdigit() or stem.split('-')[-1].isdigit():\n        return True\n\n    return False\n\n\ndef load_project_config(project_name: Optional[str] = env_project = os.environ.get(\"SCRIBE_DEFAULT_PROJECT\")\n    if env_project and env_project != project_name:\n        project = _load_project_file(PROJECTS_DIR / f\"{env_project}.json\")\n        if project:\n            return dict(project)\n\n    for path in sorted(PROJECTS_DIR.glob(\"*.json\")):\n        # Skip temp/test projects to prevent auto-switching during testing\n        if _is_temp_project(path):\n            continue\n        project = _load_project_file(path)\n        if project:\n            return dict(project)\n\n    # Final fallback: legacy single config file\n    legacy = _load_legacy_config()\n    if legacy:\n        return dict(legacy)\n    return None\n\n\ndef load_project_config_by_path(path: Path) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:\n    project = _load_project_file(path)\n    if project:\n        return dict(project)\n    return _normalise_project_data(_read_json(path), path.parent)\n\n\ndef load_config_project(project_name: Optional[str] = None) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:\n    return load_project_config(project_name)\n\n\nasync def load_active_project(state_manager: StateManager) -> Tuple[Optional[Dict[str, Any]], Optional[str], Tuple[str, ...]]:\n    state = await state_manager.load()\n    project = state.get_project(state.current_project)\n    if project:\n        return project, state.current_project, tuple(state.recent_projects)\n\n    from_config = load_project_config(state.current_project)\n    if from_config:\n        await state_manager.set_current_project(from_config[\"name\"], from_config)\n        updated_state = await state_manager.load()\n        return from_config, updated_state.current_project, tuple(updated_state.recent_projects)\n    return None, state.current_project, tuple(state.recent_projects)\n\n\ndef _load_project_file(path: Path) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:\n    if not path.exists():\n        return None\n    try:\n        mtime = path.stat().st_mtime\n    except OSError:\n        return None\n    cached = _PROJECT_CACHE.get(path)\n    if cached and cached[0] == mtime:\n        return cached[1]\n    data = _read_json(path)\n    project = _normalise_project_data(data, path.parent)\n    if project:\n        _PROJECT_CACHE[path] = (mtime, project)\n        if len(_PROJECT_CACHE) > 128:\n            _PROJECT_CACHE.pop(next(iter(_PROJECT_CACHE)))\n    return project\n\n\ndef _load_legacy_config() -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:\n    legacy_path = settings.project_root / \"config\" / "content": "\"\"\"Persistence of lightweight state for the MCP server.\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport asyncio\nimport json\nimport os\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass, field\nfrom pathlib import Path\nfrom typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional\n\nfrom scribe_mcp.config.settings import settings\nfrom scribe_mcp.utils.time import parse_utc, utcnow\n\n\nTOOL_HISTORY_LIMIT = 10\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass State:\n    current_project: Optional[str]\n    projects: Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]]\n    recent_projects: List[str]\n    session_projects: Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]] = field(default_factory=dict)\n    recent_tools: List[Dict[str, str]] = field(default_factory=list)\n    last_activity_at: Optional[str] = None\n    session_started_at: Optional[str] = None\n    version: int = 0\n    last_updated_by: Optional[str] = None\n    operation_timestamp: Optional[str] = None\n    agent_state: Dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)\n\n    def get_project(self, name: Optional[str]) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:\n        if not name:\n            return None\n        return self.projects.get(name)\n\n    def get_session_project(self, session_id: Optional[str]) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:\n        if not session_id:\n            return None\n        return self.session_projects.get(session_id)\n\n    def with_project(self, name: Optional[str], data: Optional[Dict[str, Any]]) -> \"State\":\n        projects = dict(self.projects)\n        if name and data:\n            projects[name] = data\n        recent = list(self.recent_projects)\n        if name:\n            recent = [name] + [item for item in recent if item != name]\n            limit = settings.recent_projects_limit\n            recent = recent[:limit]\n        return State(\n            current_project=name,\n            projects=projects,\n            recent_projects=recent,\n            session_projects=dict(self.session_projects),\n            recent_tools=list(self.recent_tools),\n            last_activity_at=self.last_activity_at,\n            session_started_at=self.session_started_at,\n            version=self.version,\n            last_updated_by=self.last_updated_by,\n            operation_timestamp=self.operation_timestamp,\n            agent_state=self.agent_state,\n        )\n\n\nclass StateManager:\n    \"\"\"Load and persist the server's state file.\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, path: Optional[Path] = None) -> None:\n        self._path = Path(path or settings.default_state_path)\n        self._lock = asyncio.Lock()\n        self._temp_suffix = \".tmp\"\n\n    async def load(self) -> State:\n        \"\"\"Read state from disk or return defaults.\"\"\"\n        async with self._lock:\n            data = await asyncio.to_thread(self._read_json)\n            return State(\n                current_project=data.get(\"current_project\"),\n                projects=data.get(\"projects\", {}),\n                recent_projects=data.get(\"recent_projects\", []),\n                session_projects=data.get(\"session_projects\", {}),\n                recent_tools=_normalise_tool_history(data.get(\"recent_tools\", [])),\n                last_activity_at=data.get(\"last_activity_at\"),\n                session_started_at=data.get(\"session_started_at\"),\n                version=data.get(\"version\", 0),\n                last_updated_by=data.get(\"last_updated_by\"),\n                operation_timestamp=data.get(\"operation_timestamp\"),\n                agent_state=data.get(\"agent_state\", {}),\n            )\n\n    async def persist(self, state: State) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Write the full state to disk.\"\"\"\n        await self._write_state(state)\n\n    async def record_tool(self, tool_name: str) -> State:\n        \"\"\"Track the most recent tool invocations.\"\"\"\n        async with self._lock:\n            data = await asyncio.to_thread(self._read_json)\n            now = utcnow().strftime(\"%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S UTC\")\n\n            recent_tools = _normalise_tool_history(data.get(\"recent_tools\", []))\n            filtered = [item for item in recent_tools if item.get(\"name\") != tool_name]\n            filtered.insert(0, {\"name\": tool_name, \"ts\": now})\n            limited = filtered[: TOOL_HISTORY_LIMIT]\n\n            last_activity = data.get(\"last_activity_at\")\n            session_started = data.get(\"session_started_at\")\n            reset_threshold = settings.reminder_idle_minutes\n            warm_start = session_started\n\n            if last_activity:\n                try:\n                    last_dt = parse_utc(last_activity)\n                    if last_dt:\n                        idle_minutes = (utcnow() - last_dt).total_seconds() / 60\n                        if idle_minutes >= reset_threshold:\n                            warm_start = now\n                except Exception:\n                    warm_start = now\n            else:\n                warm_start = now\n\n            data[\"recent_tools\"] = limited\n            data[\"last_activity_at\"] = now\n            data[\"session_started_at\"] = warm_start\n            await asyncio.to_thread(self._write_json, data)\n            return State(\n                current_project=data.get(\"current_project\"),\n                projects=data.get(\"projects\", {}),\n                recent_projects=data.get(\"recent_projects\", []),\n                session_projects=data.get(\"session_projects\", {}),\n                recent_tools=limited,\n project_data.setdefault(\"session_id\", agent_project.get(\"session_id\"))\n                recent_projects = await _recent_projects_snapshot(project_name)\n                return project_data, Test 4: Version conflict detection\n        print(\"  \u2713 Testing version conflict detection...\")\n        try:\n            # Try to update with wrong version\n            await agent_manager.set_current_project(\n                \"AgentA\", \"NewProject\", session_a, expected_version=999\n            )\n            print(\"    \u274c Should have detected version conflict\")\n            return False\n        except Exception as e:\n            print(f\"    \u2713 Correctly detected version conflict: {type(e).__name__}\")\n\n        # Test 5: Session validation\n        print(\"  \u2713 Testing session validation...\")\n        expired_session = await agent_manager.start_session(\"TestAgent\")\n        await agent_manager.end_session(\"TestAgent\", expired_session)\n\n        try:\n            await agent_manager.set_current_project(\"TestAgent\", \"TestProject\", expired_session)\n            print(\"    \u274c Should have rejected expired session\")\n            return False\n        except Exception as e:\n            print(f\"    \u2713 Correctly rejected expired session: {type(e).__name__}\")\n\n        await storage.close()\n\n    print(\"\u2705 set_project integration tests completed successfully!\")\n    return True\n\n\n@pytest.mark.asyncio\nasync def test_agent_context_migration():\n    \"\"\"Test legacy state migration to agent context.\"\"\"\n    print(\"\ud83e\uddea Testing legacy state migration...\")\n\n    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as temp_dir:\n        temp_path = Path(temp_dir)\n        db_path = temp_path / \"test.db\"\n        state_path = temp_path / \"state.json\"\n\n        # Initialize components\n        storage = SQLiteStorage(db_path)\n        await storage.setup()\n        state_manager = StateManager(state_path)\n\n        # Set up legacy state\n        legacy_state = await state_manager.load()\n        legacy_state.current_project = \"LegacyProject\"\n", "line_end": 120, "line_start": 1}, "frontmatter": {}, "frontmatter_byte_count": 0, "frontmatter_line_count": 0, "frontmatter_raw": "", "has_frontmatter": false, "mode": "line_range", "ok": true, "reminders": [{"category": "context", "context": "Entries:    Last: ", "emoji": "\ud83c\udfaf", "level": "info", "message": "Project: scribe_sentinel_concurrency_v1", "score": 3, "tone": "neutral"}], "scan": {"absolute_path": "/home/austin/projects/MCP_SPINE/scribe_mcp/tests/test_set_project_integration.py", "byte_size": 6074, "encoding": "utf-8", "estimated_chunk_count": 1, "line_count": 167, "newline_type": "LF", "repo_relative_path": "tests/test_set_project_integration.py", "sha256": "8eb774103bfafb7da59b9a69fa268b8f18270854aa73de17b396420db0d6f314"}}; timestamp=2026-01-03T05:46:38.792936Z; tool=read_file; priority=medium; log_type=tool_logs; content_type=log
[ℹ️] [2026-01-03 05:46:56 UTC] [Agent: Scribe] [Project: scribe_sentinel_concurrency_v1] Tool call: read_file | format_requested=readable; response_data={"chunk": {"byte_end": 4566, "byte_start": 3059, "content": "  - MCP runtime may provide a per-session identifier; if absent, router generates and persists per connection.\n- **Risks & Mitigations:**\n  - **Risk:** No session id \u2192 router generates/persists per connection; enforce validation. [Audit B]\n  - **Risk:** Concurrent append corruption \u2192 append-only JSONL + file locks; bounded retry. [Audit C]\n  - **Risk:** Mode confusion \u2192 router tool gating with clear hard-fail errors. [Audit A,C]\n<!-- ID: architecture_overview -->\n- **Solution Summary:** Introduce ExecutionContext, session-scoped state, and sentinel logging to make multi-agent work safe and auditable.\n- **Component Breakdown:**\n  - **ExecutionContext Validator:** Validates required fields and enforces mode constraints.\n  - **Session Context Store:** Keeps per-session project/sentinel context (no global leakage).\n  - **Mode Router:** Exposes only the toolset for the active mode; hard-fails on mismatches.\n  - **Sentinel Logger:** Writes daily MD + JSONL logs; supports bug/security schemas.\n  - **read_file Tool:** Reads repo files and logs provenance (sha256, agent identity).\n  - **query_entries Extension:** Adds sentinel log search by time/filters.\n- **Data Flow:** MCP call \u2192 context validation \u2192 mode router \u2192 tool execution \u2192 log append.\n- **External Integrations:** Existing SQLite registry/state, filesystem logs.\n<!-- ID: detailed_design -->\n- **ExecutionContext Authority**\n  - Router validates required fields and injects derived fields before any tool logic. [Audit B]\n", "line_end": 76, "line_start": 58}, "frontmatter": {"category": "engineering", "created_by": "Corta Labs", "doc_type": "architecture", "id": "scribe_sentinel_concurrency_v1-architecture", "last_updated": "2026-01-02", "maintained_by": "Corta Labs", "owners": [], "related_docs": [], "status": "draft", "summary": "", "tags": [], "title": "\ud83c\udfd7\ufe0f Architecture Guide \u2014 scribe_sentinel_concurrency_v1", "version": "0.1"}, "frontmatter_byte_count": 338, "frontmatter_line_count": 15, "frontmatter_raw": "---\nid: scribe_sentinel_concurrency_v1-architecture\ntitle: \"\\U0001F3D7\\uFE0F Architecture Guide \\u2014 scribe_sentinel_concurrency_v1\"\ndoc_type: architecture\ncategory: engineering\nstatus: draft\nversion: '0.1'\nlast_updated: '2026-01-02'\nmaintained_by: Corta Labs\ncreated_by: Corta Labs\nowners: []\nrelated_docs: []\ntags: []\nsummary: ''\n---\n", "has_frontmatter": true, "mode": "line_range", "ok": true, "reminders": [{"category": "context", "context": "Entries:    Last: ", "emoji": "\ud83c\udfaf", "level": "info", "message": "Project: scribe_sentinel_concurrency_v1", "score": 3, "tone": "neutral"}], "scan": {"absolute_path": "/home/austin/projects/MCP_SPINE/scribe_mcp/.scribe/docs/dev_plans/scribe_sentinel_concurrency_v1/ARCHITECTURE_GUIDE.md", "byte_size": 10036, "encoding": "utf-8", "estimated_chunk_count": 1, "line_count": 157, "newline_type": "LF", "repo_relative_path": ".scribe/docs/dev_plans/scribe_sentinel_concurrency_v1/ARCHITECTURE_GUIDE.md", "sha256": "2c0c8e86dfd5fc9084443fefe0d7143dffba41d45ddbacb9d7bc0afe73a87a9f"}}; timestamp=2026-01-03T05:46:56.141918Z; tool=read_file; priority=medium; log_type=tool_logs; content_type=log

[Audit B]\n  - Hard-fail if missing/invalid; no silent fallback. [Audit B]\n- **Session Identity Source (Final)**\n  - Router generates UUIDv4 session_id per connection and owns it. [Audit B]\n  - MCP transport identity may be recorded as transport_session_id only; never replaces router session_id. [Audit B]\n- **`current_project` Quarantine (Revision Plan #2)**\n  - Project mode resolution order: explicit ExecutionContext project \u2192 session-scoped active project \u2192 global current_project fallback. [Audit A]\n  - Sentinel mode: current_project is ignored; any fallback attempt is a hard error logged as scope_violation. [Audit A]\n  - Legacy compatibility preserved by keeping current_project intact for project mode only. [Audit A]\n- **Mode Gating (Router Boundary)**\n  - Sentinel mode exposes exactly: append_event, open_bug, open_security, link_fix, read_file, query_entries(sentinel). [Audit C,E]\n  - Project tools hard-fail when mode=sentinel; sentinel tools hard-fail when mode=project. [Audit A,C]\n  - Any new sentinel tool requires a new revision plan. [Audit C]\n- **Sentinel Write Scope**\n  - Allowed writes only under `.scribe/sentinel/YYYY-MM-DD/`; violations hard-fail and emit scope_violation event. [Audit C]\n", "line_end": 90, "line_start": 76}, "frontmatter": {"category": "engineering", "created_by": "Corta Labs", "doc_type": "architecture", "id": "scribe_sentinel_concurrency_v1-architecture", "last_updated": "2026-01-02", "maintained_by": "Corta Labs", "owners": [], "related_docs": [], "status": "draft", "summary": "", "tags": [], "title": "\ud83c\udfd7\ufe0f Architecture Guide \u2014 scribe_sentinel_concurrency_v1", "version": "0.1"}, "frontmatter_byte_count": 338, "frontmatter_line_count": 15, "frontmatter_raw": "---\nid: scribe_sentinel_concurrency_v1-architecture\ntitle: \"\\U0001F3D7\\uFE0F Architecture Guide \\u2014 scribe_sentinel_concurrency_v1\"\ndoc_type: architecture\ncategory: engineering\nstatus: draft\nversion: '0.1'\nlast_updated: '2026-01-02'\nmaintained_by: Corta Labs\ncreated_by: Corta Labs\nowners: []\nrelated_docs: []\ntags: []\nsummary: ''\n---\n", "has_frontmatter": true, "mode": "line_range", "ok": true, "reminders": [{"category": "context", "context": "Entries:    Last: ", "emoji": "\ud83c\udfaf", "level": "info", "message": "Project: scribe_sentinel_concurrency_v1", "score": 3, "tone": "neutral"}], "scan": {"absolute_path": "/home/austin/projects/MCP_SPINE/scribe_mcp/.scribe/docs/dev_plans/scribe_sentinel_concurrency_v1/ARCHITECTURE_GUIDE.md", "byte_size": 10036, "encoding": "utf-8", "estimated_chunk_count": 1, "line_count": 157, "newline_type": "LF", "repo_relative_path": ".scribe/docs/dev_plans/scribe_sentinel_concurrency_v1/ARCHITECTURE_GUIDE.md", "sha256": "2c0c8e86dfd5fc9084443fefe0d7143dffba41d45ddbacb9d7bc0afe73a87a9f"}}; timestamp=2026-01-03T05:46:59.776097Z; tool=read_file; priority=medium; log_type=tool_logs; content_type=log

[Audit B]\n  - **Risk:** Concurrent append corruption \u2192 append-only JSONL + file locks; bounded retry. [Audit C]\n  - **Risk:** Mode confusion \u2192 router tool gating with clear hard-fail errors. [Audit A,C]\n<!-- ID: architecture_overview -->\n- **Solution Summary:** Introduce ExecutionContext, session-scoped state, and sentinel logging to make multi-agent work safe and auditable.\n- **Component Breakdown:**\n  - **ExecutionContext Validator:** Validates required fields and enforces mode constraints.\n  - **Session Context Store:** Keeps per-session project/sentinel context (no global leakage).\n  - **Mode Router:** Exposes only the toolset for the active mode; [Audit D]\n- **Non-Functional Requirements:**\n  - Concurrency-safe append semantics for JSON logs; MD remains single-line under lock.\n  - Deterministic path validation (hard-fail on scope mismatch).\n  - Backwards-compatible project-mode behavior.\n- **Assumptions:**\n  - Repo root is set via `set_project` with absolute path.\n  - MCP runtime must provide a per-session transport_session_id (or explicit session_id); router hard-fails if missing.\n- **Risks & Mitigations:**\n  - **Risk:** Missing session id \u2192 hard-fail at router; clients must supply transport_session_id or session_id. [Audit B]\n  - **Risk:** Concurrent append corruption \u2192 append-only JSONL + file locks; bounded retry. [ℹ️] [2026-01-03 05:50:15 UTC] [Agent: Scribe] [Project: scribe_sentinel_concurrency_v1] Tool call: read_file | format_requested=readable; response_data={"chunk": {"byte_end": 5007, "byte_start": 3902, "content": "  - **Mode Router:** Exposes only the toolset for the active mode; hard-fails on mismatches.\n  - **Sentinel Logger:** Writes daily MD + JSONL logs; supports bug/security schemas.\n  - **read_file Tool:** Reads repo files and logs provenance (sha256, agent identity).\n  - **query_entries Extension:** Adds sentinel log search by time/filters.\n- **Data Flow:** MCP call \u2192 context validation \u2192 mode router \u2192 tool execution \u2192 log append.\n  - MCP runtime must provide a per-session transport_session_id (or explicit session_id); router hard-fails if missing.\n- **Risks & Mitigations:**\n  - **Risk:** Missing session id \u2192 hard-fail at router; clients must supply transport_session_id or session_id. [Audit B]\n  - Router validates required fields and injects derived fields before any tool logic. [Audit B]\n  - Hard-fail if missing/invalid; no silent fallback. [Audit B]\n- **Session Identity Source (Final)**\n  - Router generates UUIDv4 session_id per connection and owns it. [Audit B]\n  - MCP transport identity may be recorded as transport_session_id only; never replaces router session_id. [Audit B]\n", "line_end": 80, "line_start": 68}, "frontmatter": {"category": "engineering", "created_by": "Corta Labs", "doc_type": "architecture", "id": "scribe_sentinel_concurrency_v1-architecture", "last_updated": "2026-01-03", "maintained_by": "Corta Labs", "owners": [], "related_docs": [], "status": "draft", "summary": "", "tags": [], "title": "\ud83c\udfd7\ufe0f Architecture Guide \u2014 scribe_sentinel_concurrency_v1", "version": "0.1"}, "frontmatter_byte_count": 338, "frontmatter_line_count": 15, "frontmatter_raw": "---\nid: scribe_sentinel_concurrency_v1-architecture\ntitle: \"\\U0001F3D7\\uFE0F Architecture Guide \\u2014 scribe_sentinel_concurrency_v1\"\ndoc_type: architecture\ncategory: engineering\nstatus: draft\nversion: '0.1'\nlast_updated: '2026-01-03'\nmaintained_by: Corta Labs\ncreated_by: Corta Labs\nowners: []\nrelated_docs: []\ntags: []\nsummary: ''\n---\n", "has_frontmatter": true, "mode": "line_range", "ok": true, "reminders": [{"category": "context", "context": "Entries:    Last: ", "emoji": "\ud83c\udfaf", "level": "info", "message": "Project: scribe_sentinel_concurrency_v1", "score": 3, "tone": "neutral"}], "scan": {"absolute_path": "/home/austin/projects/MCP_SPINE/scribe_mcp/.scribe/docs/dev_plans/scribe_sentinel_concurrency_v1/ARCHITECTURE_GUIDE.md", "byte_size": 10181, "encoding": "utf-8", "estimated_chunk_count": 1, "line_count": 157, "newline_type": "LF", "repo_relative_path": ".scribe/docs/dev_plans/scribe_sentinel_concurrency_v1/ARCHITECTURE_GUIDE.md", "sha256": "4904c8f458f09cc22aeea820c62bccce257593e6e101aae504523597fa3db0cf"}}; timestamp=2026-01-03T05:50:14.985738Z; tool=read_file; priority=medium; log_type=tool_logs; content_type=log
[ℹ️] [2026-01-03 05:50:35 UTC] [Agent: Scribe] [Project: scribe_sentinel_concurrency_v1] Tool call: read_file | format_requested=readable; response_data={"chunk": {"byte_end": 3010, "byte_start": 1433, "content": "<!-- ID: requirements_constraints -->\n- **Functional Requirements:**\n  - ExecutionContext required on every tool call at router boundary (repo_root, mode, execution_id, agent_identity, intent, timestamp_utc, affected_dev_projects; sentinel_day derived in sentinel mode). [Audit A,B]\n  - Missing/invalid ExecutionContext = hard-fail with explicit error response. [Audit B]\n  - Execution/session identity has a single authoritative source at router boundary; no fallback to global state. [Audit B]\n  - Sentinel logging writes only under `.scribe/sentinel/YYYY-MM-DD/` (MD + JSONL). [Audit C]\n  - `read_file` logs read provenance in sentinel mode (JSONL required; MD optional). [Audit E]\n  - Session-scoped context prevents cross-agent leakage; project mode uses ordered resolution. [Audit A]\n  - `current_project` remains for project mode fallback only; forbidden in sentinel mode with hard error. [Audit A]\n  - `query_entries` can search sentinel logs by time, execution_id, agent identity, intent, dev_project tags, bug/security IDs, and file paths. [Audit D]\n  - Doc registry keys are canonical, case-insensitive, and aliasable; errors list known doc keys. [Audit E]\n  - `query_entries` can search sentinel logs by time, execution_id, agent identity, intent, dev_project tags, bug/security IDs, and file paths. [Audit A]\n  - Agents can re-connect to old projects via fingerprint (agent_identity + session_id + repo_root), session-scoped lookup preferred.
